    var path = (window.location.pathname || '/').split('#')[0].split('?')[0];
    if (path.endsWith('/index')) path = path.slice(0, -6);
    else if (path === 'index') path = '';

    if (path) candidates.push(path);

    else candidates.push('/' + path);

    var item = null;
    for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length && !item; i++) {
      var matches = document.querySelectorAll('[id="' + candidates[i].replace(/"/g, '\\"') + '"]');
      for (var j = 0; j < matches.length; j++) {
        if (matches[j].closest('#sidebar, #sidebar-content')) {
          item = matches[j];

    if (!item) return;

    var parent = item.closest('[data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport"]');
    if (!parent) {
      parent = item.parentElement;
      while (parent) {
        var style = getComputedStyle(parent);
        if (style.overflowY === 'auto' || style.overflowY === 'scroll') break;
        parent = parent.parentElement;
      }

    var parentRect = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var itemRect = item.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (itemRect.top >= parentRect.top && itemRect.bottom <= parentRect.bottom) return;

    var itemTopRelative = itemRect.top - parentRect.top + parent.scrollTop;
    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;
  } catch (e) {}

data-custom-css-path="style.css">#content-area {
  --font-mono: var(--font-jetbrains-mono), ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, "Liberation Mono", "Courier New", monospace; /* via Mintlify theme */

  h5 {
    font-weight: 500;
  }

  h6 {
    font-weight: 400;
  }

  /* Fix code block line highlight color */
  code .line-highlight {
    --primary-light: 100 175 250;
  }
}



/*** Add automatic section numbers to headings and table of contents items ***/

#enable-section-numbers {
  display: none;
}

body:has(#enable-section-numbers) {
  #content-area,
  #table-of-contents {
    counter-reset: h2-counter h3-counter h4-counter h5-counter h6-counter;
  }

  #content-area h2[id],
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="0"] {
    counter-increment: h2-counter;
    counter-set: h3-counter h4-counter h5-counter h6-counter;
  }

  #content-area h3[id],
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="1"] {
    counter-increment: h3-counter;
    counter-set: h4-counter h5-counter h6-counter;
  }

  #content-area h4[id],
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="2"] {
    counter-increment: h4-counter;
    counter-set: h5-counter h6-counter;
  }

  #content-area h5[id],
  #content-area h5,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="3"] {
    counter-increment: h5-counter;
    counter-set: h6-counter;
  }

  #content-area h6[id],
  #content-area h6,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="4"] {
    counter-increment: h6-counter;
  }

  #table-of-contents li a::before {
    flex-shrink: 0;
  }

  #content-area h2[id]::before,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="0"] a::before {
    content: counter(h2-counter) ". ";
  }

  #content-area h3[id]::before,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="1"] a::before {
    content: counter(h2-counter) "." counter(h3-counter) " ";
  }

  #content-area h4[id]::before,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="2"] a::before {
    content: counter(h2-counter) "." counter(h3-counter) "." counter(h4-counter)
      " ";
  }

  #content-area h5[id]::before,
  #content-area h5::before,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="3"] a::before {
    content: counter(h2-counter) "." counter(h3-counter) "." counter(h4-counter)
      "." counter(h5-counter) " ";
  }

  #content-area h6[id]::before,
  #content-area h6::before,
  #table-of-contents li[data-depth="4"] a::before {
    content: counter(h2-counter) "." counter(h3-counter) "." counter(h4-counter)
      "." counter(h5-counter) "." counter(h6-counter) " ";
  }
}



/*** Page: schema reference ***/

#schema-reference {
  display: none;
}

/* Vertical margins */
body:has(#schema-reference) {
  * {
    scroll-margin-top: 8rem;
  }

  --mt-sm: 0.25rem;
  --mt-md: 0.5rem;
  --mt-lg: 1rem;
  --mt-xl: 1.5rem;

  /* Headings */
  h2, h3 {
    margin: 0;
  }
  .type + h2, .type h3 {
    margin-top: calc(5 * var(--mt-lg));
  }
  h2 + .type h3 {
    margin-top: var(--mt-xl);
  }

  /* Type definition code */
  .tsd-signature {
    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;
  }

  /* Prose */
  .tsd-comment {
    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;

    p, ul, ol, li {
      margin: 0;
    }

    * + :is(p, ul, ol, pre) {
      margin-top: var(--mt-lg);
    }

    * + li {
      margin-top: var(--mt-sm);
    }
  }

  /* Example block (`@example` tag) */
  .tsd-tag-example {
    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;

    summary + * {
      margin: var(--mt-sm) 0 0 0;
      scroll-margin-top: calc(8rem + 1.5rem + var(--mt-sm));
    }
  }

  /* "See" links (`@see` tag) */
  .tsd-tag-see {
    & > :is(p, ul) {
      margin-top: 0;
    }
  }

  /* Type member */
  .tsd-member {
    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;

    /* Description */
    .tsd-comment {
      margin-top: var(--mt-sm);
    }

    /* Sub-members */
    .tsd-type-declaration {
      /* List */
      [data-typedoc-h="4"] + .tsd-parameters {
        margin: var(--mt-md) 0 0 0;
      }
    }

    /* Example (`@example` tag) inside type member */
    .tsd-tag-example {
      margin: var(--mt-sm) 0 0 0;
    }
  }
}

body:has(#schema-reference) {
  /* Prose */
  .tsd-comment {
    a.tsd-kind-type-alias,
    a.tsd-kind-interface,
    a.tsd-kind-property {
      font-family: var(--font-mono);
      font-size: 0.875em;
    }
  }

  /* Code blocks */
  .tsd-signature,
  .tsd-tag-example pre:has(code) {
    /* Based on code blocks rendered by Mintlify. */
    border: 1px solid;
    border-color: light-dark(rgb(var(--gray-950)/.1), rgba(255 255 255/0.1));
    border-radius: 1rem;
    padding: 1rem 0.875rem;

    background-color: inherit;
    color: inherit;
    font-family: var(--font-mono);
    font-size: 0.875rem;
    line-height: 1.5rem;
  }

  /* Type definition code */
  .tsd-signature {
    a {
      font-weight: normal;
      border-bottom: none;
      text-decoration: underline;

      &:hover {
        text-decoration-thickness: 2px;
      }
    }

    a[href="#"] {
      pointer-events: none;
      color: inherit;
      text-decoration: none;
    }

    .tsd-signature-keyword {
      color: light-dark(rgb(207, 34, 46), #9CDCFE);
    }

    :is(.tsd-kind-interface, .tsd-kind-type-alias):not(.tsd-signature-type) {
      color: light-dark(rgb(149, 56, 0), #4EC9B0);
    }

    .tsd-signature-type:not(.tsd-kind-interface, .tsd-kind-type-alias) {
      color: light-dark(rgb(5, 80, 174), #DCDCAA);
    }
  }

  /* Example block (`@example` tag) */
  .tsd-tag-example {
    /* Label */
    summary {
      font-weight: 600;
      font-style: italic;
    }

    /* Code */
    pre code.json {
      .hl-1 {
        color: light-dark(rgb(17, 99, 41), #9CDCFE);
      }
      .hl-2, .hl-3 {
        color: light-dark(rgb(10, 48, 105), #CE9178);
      }
      .hl-5 {
        color: light-dark(rgb(5, 80, 174), #569CD6);
      }
    }

    /* Copy button */
    pre button {
      display: none;
    }
  }

  /* "See" links (`@see` tag) */
  .tsd-tag-see {
    /* Add trailing ":" to heading */
    [data-typedoc-h="4"]::after {
      content: ": ";
    }

    /* Hide (defunct) anchor link */
    .tsd-anchor-icon {
      display: none;
    }

    /* Case: single link -- display inline with heading */
    &:has(> p) {
      [data-typedoc-h="4"], p {
        display: inline;
      }
    }
  }

  /* Type member */
  .tsd-member {
    /* Hide members that don't have doc comments */
    &:not(:has(.tsd-comment)) {
      display: none;
    }

    /* When the type itself has a doc comment, add a subtle top border to the first visible member */
    .tsd-comment ~ &:has(.tsd-comment):not(.tsd-member:has(.tsd-comment) ~ *) {
      border-top: 2px solid light-dark(rgb(var(--gray-950)/.05), rgba(255 255 255/0.1));
      padding-top: var(--mt-md);
    }

    /* Member name */
    [data-typedoc-h="3"] {
      font-family: var(--font-mono);
      font-weight: 700;
    }

    & > .tsd-comment,
    & > .tsd-type-declaration {
      margin-left: 1.25rem;
    }

    /* Sub-members */
    .tsd-type-declaration {
      /* Templated heading ("Type Declaration") */
      [data-typedoc-h="4"] {
        display: none;
      }

      /* Name and type */
      [data-typedoc-h="5"] {
        font-family: var(--font-mono);
        font-size: 0.875rem;
        font-weight: 500;

        width: fit-content;
        padding: 0.125em 0.5em;
        background-color: light-dark(rgb(var(--gray-100)/.5), rgb(255 255 255/.05));

        .tsd-tag {
          display: none;
        }
      }
    }

    /* Example (`@example` tag) inside type member */
    .tsd-tag-example {
      padding-left: 0.5rem;
    }

    .tsd-sources {
      display: none;
    }
  }

  /* Anchor links for members and examples */
  .tsd-anchor-icon {
    border: 0;
    padding: 0 0.5rem;
    color: rgb(var(--gray-500)/.5);

    &:hover {
      color: rgb(var(--gray-500))
    }

    &::before {
      content: "#";
      color: inherit;
    }

    svg {
      display: none;
    }
  }

  /* Types with only index properties (e.g., `type Foo = { [key: string]: unknown }`) */
  .type > .tsd-type-declaration {
    /* Hide property if no doc comments */
    &:not(:has(.tsd-comment)) {
      display: none;
    }

    /* Templated heading ("Type Declaration") */
    [data-typedoc-h="4"] {
      display: none;
    }

    /* Index property */
    [data-typedoc-h="5"] {
      font-family: var(--font-mono);
      font-weight: 700;
    }
  }
}



/*** LF Projects copyright footer ***/

/* footer.js appends #lf-copyright into Mintlify's #footer flex row, which
   already draws the top border. Let the row wrap so the notice sits beside
   the social icons when there is room and drops to its own line otherwise. */
#footer {
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  row-gap: 1.5rem;
}

#lf-copyright {
  flex: 1 1 32rem;
  min-width: 0;
  text-align: center;
  font-size: 0.875rem;
  color: rgb(var(--gray-500));
  line-height: 1.5;

  a {
    color: inherit;
    text-decoration: underline;

    &:hover {
      color: rgb(var(--gray-700));
    }
  }
}

stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Prerequisites</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">You’ll need <a href="https://nodejs.org/en/download" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Node.js</a> 18 or higher. Familiarity
with <a class="link" href="/specification/latest/server/tools">MCP tools</a> and
<a class="link" href="/specification/latest/server/resources">resources</a> is recommended since MCP
Apps combine both primitives. Experience with the
<a href="https://github.com/modelcontextprotocol/typescript-sdk" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">MCP TypeScript SDK</a>
will help you better understand the server-side patterns.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="getting-started"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#getting-started" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Getting started</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The fastest way to create an MCP App is using an AI coding agent with the MCP
Apps skill. If you prefer to set up a project manually, skip to
<a href="#manual-setup" class="link">Manual setup</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="using-an-ai-coding-agent"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#using-an-ai-coding-agent" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Using an AI coding agent</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">AI coding agents with Skills support can scaffold a complete MCP App project for
you. Skills are folders of instructions and resources that your agent loads when
relevant. They teach the AI how to perform specialized tasks like creating MCP

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="building-an-mcp-app"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#building-an-mcp-app" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Building an MCP App</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Let’s build a simple app that displays the current server time. This example
demonstrates the full pattern: registering a tool with UI metadata, serving the
bundled HTML as a resource, and building a UI that communicates with the server.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="server-implementation"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#server-implementation" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Server implementation</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The server needs to do two things: register a tool that includes the
<code>_meta.ui.resourceUri</code> field, and register a resource handler that serves the
bundled HTML. Here’s the complete server file:</span>

<span data-as="p">Let’s break down the key parts:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>resourceUri</code></strong>: The <code>ui://</code> scheme tells hosts this is an MCP App resource.
The path structure is arbitrary.</li>
<li><strong><code>registerAppTool</code></strong>: Registers a tool with the <code>_meta.ui.resourceUri</code> field.
When the host calls this tool, the UI is fetched and rendered, and the tool result is passed to it upon arrival.</li>
<li><strong><code>registerAppResource</code></strong>: Serves the bundled HTML when the host requests the UI resource.</li>
<li><strong>Express server</strong>: Exposes the MCP server over HTTP on port 3001.</li>
</ul>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="ui-implementation"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#ui-implementation" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">UI implementation</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The UI consists of an HTML page and a TypeScript module that uses the <code>App</code>
class to communicate with the host. Here’s the HTML:</span>

<span data-as="p">The key parts:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>app.connect()</code></strong>: Establishes communication with the host. Call this once
when your app initializes.</li>
<li><strong><code>app.ontoolresult</code></strong>: A callback that fires when the host pushes a tool
result to your app (e.g., when the tool is first called and the UI renders).</li>
<li><strong><code>app.callServerTool()</code></strong>: Lets your app proactively call tools on the server.
Keep in mind that each call involves a round-trip to the server, so design your
UI to handle latency gracefully.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The <code>App</code> class provides additional methods for logging, opening URLs, and
updating the model’s context with structured data from your app. See the full
<a href="https://apps.extensions.modelcontextprotocol.io/api/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">API documentation</a>.</span>

<span data-as="p">In the default configuration, your server will be available at
<code>http://localhost:3001/mcp</code>. However, to see your app render, you need an MCP
host that supports MCP Apps. You have several options.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="testing-with-claude"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#testing-with-claude" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Testing with Claude</span></h3>
<span data-as="p"><a href="https://claude.ai" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude</a> (web) and <a href="https://claude.ai/download" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Claude Desktop</a>
support MCP Apps. For local development, you’ll need to expose your server to
the internet. You can run an MCP server locally and use tools like <code>cloudflared</code>
to tunnel traffic through.</span>
<span data-as="p">In a separate terminal, run:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">Copy the generated URL (e.g., <code>https://random-name.trycloudflare.com</code>) and add it
as a <a href="https://support.anthropic.com/en/articles/11175166-getting-started-with-custom-connectors-using-remote-mcp" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">custom connector</a>
in Claude - click on your profile, go to <strong>Settings</strong>, <strong>Connectors</strong>, and
finally <strong>Add custom connector</strong>.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><path d="M 9 1.6714 C 13.0371 1.6714 16.3286 4.9629 16.3286 9 C 16.3286 13.0371 13.0371 16.3286 9 16.3286 C 7.0574 16.3252 5.1953 15.552 3.8217 14.1783 C 2.448 12.8047 1.6748 10.9426 1.6714 9 C 1.6714 4.9629 4.9629 1.6714 9 1.6714 Z M 9 0 C 4.0371 0 0 4.0371 0 9 C 0 13.9629 4.0371 18 9 18 C 13.9629 18 18 13.9629 18 9 C 18 4.0371 13.9629 0 9 0 Z M 10.2857 3.8571 H 7.7143 V 10.2857 H 10.2857 V 3.8571 Z M 10.2857 11.5714 H 7.7143 V 14.1429 H 10.2857 V 11.5714 Z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none" fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! test interface. To connect it to a specific server (e.g., one you’re developing),
pass the <code>SERVERS</code> environment variable inline:</span>

<span data-as="p">Navigate to <code>http://localhost:8080</code>. You’ll see a simple interface where you can
select a tool and call it. When you call your tool, the host fetches the UI
resource and renders it in a sandboxed iframe. You can then interact with your
app and verify that tool calls work correctly.</span>
<div><figure data-name="frame" class="frame p-2 not-prose relative bg-gray-50/50 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden dark:bg-gray-800/25 print:print-color-exact"><div style="background-position:10px 10px" --mt-sm: 0.25rem;\n  --mt-md: 0.5rem;\n  --mt-lg: 1rem;\n  --mt-xl: 1.5rem;\n\n  /* Headings */\n  h2, h3 {\n    margin: 0;\n  }\n  .type + h2, .type h3 {\n    margin-top: calc(5 * var(--mt-lg));\n  }\n  h2 + .type h3 {\n    margin-top: var(--mt-xl);\n  }\n\n  /* Type definition code */\n  .tsd-signature {\n    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;\n  }\n\n  /* Prose */\n  .tsd-comment {\n    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;\n\n    p, ul, ol, li {\n      margin: 0;\n    }\n\n    * + :is(p, ul, ol, pre) {\n      margin-top: var(--mt-lg);\n    }\n\n    * + li {\n      margin-top: var(--mt-sm);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Example block (`@example` tag) */\n  .tsd-tag-example {\n    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;\n\n    summary + * {\n      margin: var(--mt-sm) 0 0 0;\n      scroll-margin-top: calc(8rem + 1.5rem + var(--mt-sm));\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* \"See\" links (`@see` tag) */\n  .tsd-tag-see {\n    \u0026 \u003e :is(p, ul) {\n      margin-top: 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Type member */\n  .tsd-member {\n    margin: var(--mt-lg) 0 0 0;\n\n    /* Description */\n    .tsd-comment {\n      margin-top: var(--mt-sm);\n    }\n\n    /* Sub-members */\n    .tsd-type-declaration {\n      /* List */\n var(--mt-sm) 0 0 0;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nbody:has(#schema-reference) {\n  /* Prose */\n  .tsd-comment {\n    a.tsd-kind-type-alias,\n    a.tsd-kind-interface,\n    a.tsd-kind-property {\n      font-family: var(--font-mono);\n      font-size: 0.875em;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Code blocks */\n  .tsd-signature,\n  .tsd-tag-example pre:has(code) {\n    /* Based on code blocks rendered by Mintlify. */\n    border: 1px solid;\n    border-color: light-dark(rgb(var(--gray-950)/.1), rgba(255 255 255/0.1));\n    border-radius: 1rem;\n    padding: 1rem 0.875rem;\n\n    background-color: inherit;\n    color: inherit;\n    font-family: var(--font-mono);\n    font-size: 0.875rem;\n    line-height: 1.5rem;\n  }\n\n  /* Type definition code */\n  .tsd-signature {\n    a {\n      font-weight: normal;\n color: inherit;\n      text-decoration: none;\n    }\n\n    .tsd-signature-keyword {\n      color: light-dark(rgb(207, 34, 46), #9CDCFE);\n    }\n\n    :is(.tsd-kind-interface, .tsd-kind-type-alias):not(.tsd-signature-type) {\n      color: light-dark(rgb(149, 56, 0), #4EC9B0);\n    }\n\n    .tsd-signature-type:not(.tsd-kind-interface, .tsd-kind-type-alias) {\n      color: light-dark(rgb(5, 80, 174), #DCDCAA);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Example block (`@example` tag) */\n  .tsd-tag-example {\n    /* Label */\n    summary {\n      font-weight: 600;\n      font-style: italic;\n    }\n\n    /* Code */\n    pre code.json {\n      .hl-1 {\n        color: light-dark(rgb(17, 99, 41), #9CDCFE);\n      }\n      .hl-2, .hl-3 {\n        color: light-dark(rgb(10, 48, 105), #CE9178);\n      }\n      .hl-5 {\n        color: light-dark(rgb(5, 80, 174), #569CD6);\n      }\n    }\n\n    /* Copy button */\n    pre button {\n      display: none;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* \"See\" links (`@see` tag) */\n  .tsd-tag-see {\n    /* Add trailing \":\" to heading */\n    [data-typedoc-h=\"4\"]::after {\n      content: \": \";\n    }\n\n    /* Hide (defunct) anchor link */\n    .tsd-anchor-icon {\n      display: none;\n    }\n\n    /* Case: single link -- display inline with heading */\n    \u0026:has(\u003e p) {\n      [data-typedoc-h=\"4\"], p {\n        display: inline;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /* Type member */\n  .tsd-member {\n    /* Hide members that don't have doc comments */\n    \u0026:not(:has(.tsd-comment)) {\n      display: none;\n    }\n\n    /* When the type itself has a /* Templated heading (\"Type Declaration\") */\n      [data-typedoc-h=\"4\"] {\n        display: none;\n      }\n\n      /* Name and type */\n      [data-typedoc-h=\"5\"] {\n        font-family: var(--font-mono);\n        font-size: 0.875rem;\n        font-weight: 500;\n\n        width: fit-content;\n        padding: 0.125em 0.5em;\n        background-color: light-dark(rgb(var(--gray-100)/.5), rgb(255 255 255/.05));\n\n        .tsd-tag {\n          display: none;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    /* Example (`@example` tag) inside type member */\n property if no doc comments */\n    \u0026:not(:has(.tsd-comment)) {\n      display: none;\n    }\n\n    /* Templated heading (\"Type Declaration\") */\n    [data-typedoc-h=\"4\"] {\n      display: none;\n    }\n\n    /* Index property */\n    [data-typedoc-h=\"5\"] {\n      font-family: var(--font-mono);\n      font-weight: 700;\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n\n\n/*** LF Projects copyright footer ***/\n\n/* contentArea.insertBefore(banner, contentArea.firstChild);\n    }\n  } finally {\n    inserting = false;\n  }\n}\n\nif (document.readyState === \"loading\") {\n
